And he cannot first remove the blood from the wound, on account of chovel, as was explained above. / Rather, how should he act? … and the whole wound, and afterwards wraps a rag (smartut) around it. And some have misgivings and forbid wrapping cobwebs, because they heal; rather he should first wash with water or with wine to remove the blood that is on the wound, and afterwards wrap the rag around it. And it is good to be concerned for their words, but the main [ruling] is like the first opinion
- **séif 54, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « ובמילה לא התירו אלא מפני הסכנה ולכן אסור למצוץ דם שבין השנים (ואף על פי שהוא מפרק כלאחר יד…) | ואף על פי שמושך מעצמו… כחובל ומפרק בידיו » → and at a circumcision (milah) they permitted it only because of the danger. And therefore it is forbidden to suck the blood that is between the teeth (and even though it is mefarek ki-le'achar yad, nonetheless it is forbidden by the words of the Sages). / …And even though it draws of itself, nonetheless, since he places it there in order to draw — this is like wounding and extracting with his hands
- **séif 55, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « כדרך שנוהגים לעשות למי שהוא עצור | שיאחזנה בשתי אצבעותיו ויניחנה בנחת משום השרת נימין כמו שנתבאר בסימן שי״ב | אפלו הכינה מאתמול (משום גזרת שחיקת סמנים) | וגם בחולה צריך לזהר שלא יבא לידי מלאכה גמורה של תורה (וכן) » → in the manner that is customarily done for one who is constipated … holding it with two of his fingers and placing it gently — because of the removal of hairs (hasharas nimin), as was explained in siman 312. / …even if he prepared it the day before (because of the decree of grinding ingredients) … And even with a choleh one must take care not to come to a full Torah-level melachah; (and likewise)

### Siman 329

- **séif 1, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « כְּגוֹן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ חוֹלֶה אוֹ קְטַנִּים וְאֵין שָׁהוּת לְהַבְרִיחָם טֶרֶם שֶׁתַּגִּיעַ לָהּ הַדְּלֵקָה » → for example, there is a sick person or young children in it, and there is no time to make them flee before the fire reaches it
- **séif 1, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « דֶּרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים גְּמוּרָה » → through a full-fledged reshus ha-rabbim (public domain); replaces the earlier gloss « (the children, etc.) »
- **séif 1, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִימָן רע״ח, וְהוֹצָאַת חוֹלֶה אוֹ קְטַנִּים שֶׁאֵינָם יְכוֹלִים לֵילֵךְ הִיא מְלָאכָה גְמוּרָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִימָן ש״ח » → as explained in Siman 278; whereas carrying out a sick person or young children who cannot walk is a full-fledged melachah, as explained in Siman 308
- **séif 2, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « בְּאוֹתָהּ חָצֵר וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא אִם נָכְרִי … אֶת הַגָּל … בְּאוֹתָהּ חָצֵר » → in that same courtyard, and it is not known whether he is a Jew or a non-Jew — we clear the rubble (mefakchin alav es ha-gal) … fixed (kevu'im) in that same courtyard
- **séif 2, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ פֵּרֵשׁ אֶחָד מֵהֶם לְחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת וְשָׁם נָפְלָה עָלָיו מַפֹּלֶת, שֶׁהָיָה רָאוּי לֵילֵךְ אַחַר הָרֹב — אַף עַל פִּי כֵן מְפַקְּחִין עָלָיו, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּשְׁאָר קְבִיעוּת הָרִאשׁוֹן בִּמְקוֹמוֹ (בִּשְׁעַת פְּרִישָׁתוֹ מֵהַקְּבִיעוּת) — הֲרֵי אָנוּ חוֹשְׁבִין אוֹתוֹ כְּקָבוּעַ עִמָּהֶם לְהָקֵל בִּסְפֵק נְפָשׁוֹת » → Moreover: even if one of them separated to another courtyard and a heap of rubble fell on him there — where it would have been proper to follow the majority — we nevertheless clear the rubble for him: since the original fixed group (keviyus) remains in its place (at the time he separated from the fixed group), we regard him as kavua with them, to be lenient in a doubt of lives.
- **séif 2, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « בְּזֶה אַחַר זֶה … לְחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת וְנָפְלָה עָלָיו מַפֹּלֶת … שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּעֵר כְּבָר קְבִיעוּת הָרִאשׁוֹן מִמְּקוֹמוֹ (בִּשְׁעַת פְּרִישָׁתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה) — אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהָקֵל בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם קָבוּעַ, וְהוֹלְכִין בּוֹ אַחַר הָרֹב שֶׁהֵם נָכְרִים » → one after another … to another courtyard and a heap of rubble fell on him … since the original fixed group has already been shaken loose from its place (at the time this one separated), it is impossible to be lenient with him on account of kavua, and we follow the majority with him, who are non-Jews
- **séif 2, EN** (portée depuis la page FR; « [at once] » is a bracketed editorial addition (second pass), not in the Hebrew) — « אֲבָל אִם נֶעֶקְרוּ כֻלָּם — הֲרֵי עֲדַיִן הֵם קְבוּעִים בְּיַחַד, וּכְשֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ אֶחָד מֵהֶם — הֲרֵי פֵּרֵשׁ מֵהַקָּבוּעַ, וּמְפַקְּחִין עָלָיו » → But if they all dispersed [at once] — they are still fixed together, and when one of them separates, he has separated from the fixed group, and we clear the rubble for him.
- **séif 2, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « וְכֵן תִּינוֹק שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְּעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ נָכְרִים — יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁאֵין מְחַלְּלִין עָלָיו אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, כֵּיוָן שֶׁבְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם פּוֹרְשִׁים כֻּלָּם מִמְּקוֹם קְבִיעוּתָם — הוֹלְכִין אַחַר הָרֹב (כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּאֶבֶן הָעֶזֶר סִימָן ד׳, עַיֵּן שָׁם שֶׁיֵּשׁ חוֹלְקִין) » → And likewise an infant found in a city whose majority is non-Jewish — there are those who say that we do not desecrate Shabbos for him, since every single day they all separate from their fixed place — we follow the majority (as written in Even ha-Ezer Siman 4; see there, for some disagree)
- **séif 3, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ כַּמָּה סְפֵקוֹת, שֶׁהֲרֵי אָמְרָה תוֹרָה "וָחַי בָּהֶם", שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לָבֹא בְּשׁוּם עִנְיָן לִידֵי מִיתַת יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל יְדֵי שְׁמִירַת הַמִּצְווֹת » → even though there are several doubts in it; for the Torah said "va-chai bahem" ("and he shall live by them") — that one should in no way come to the death of a Jew through the observance of the mitzvos
- **séif 3, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « מְרֻצָּץ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִחְיוֹת אֶלָּא לְפִי שָׁעָה … אִם יֵשׁ בּוֹ חַיּוּת — מְפַקְּחִים וּמוֹצִיאִים אוֹתוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל חַיֵּי שָׁעָה, וְאִם לֹא הִרְגִּישׁוּ בְּחָטְמוֹ חַיּוּת — אָז וַדַּאי כְּבָר מֵת, אֲפִלּוּ אֵינוֹ מְרֻצָּץ » → (meruttzatz), so that he cannot live except momentarily … if there is life in him — they clear and take him out for the sake of momentary life (chayei sha'ah); and if they did not sense life at his nose — then he is certainly already dead, even if he is not crushed
- **séif 3, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « בֵּין שֶׁפָּגְעוּ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ תְּחִלָּה וּבֵין שֶׁפָּגְעוּ בְּרַגְלָיו תְּחִלָּה, וְרָאוּ שֶׁאֵין חַיּוּת בְּלִבּוֹ — אַף עַל פִּי כֵן בּוֹדְקִין עַד חָטְמוֹ, כִּי עִקַּר הֶכֵּר חַיּוּת הוּא בְּחֹטֶם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "כֹּל אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁמַת רוּחַ חַיִּים בְּאַפָּיו" » → Whether they reached his head first or reached his feet first, and they saw that there is no life in his heart — they nevertheless check up to his nose, for the principal sign of life is in the nose, as it is said: "kol asher nishmas ruach chayim be-apav" ("all in whose nostrils is the breath of the spirit of life")
- **séif 4, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁמָּא הֵם חַיִּים. מַעֲשֶׂה הָיָה וּמָצְאוּ עֶלְיוֹנִים מֵתִים וְתַחְתּוֹנִים » → alive. It once happened that they found the upper ones dead and the lower ones [alive]
- **séif 5, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « שָׁם … (שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין מְצֻוִּין לְהַחֲיוֹתוֹ) » → there … (for we are not commanded to keep him alive)
- **séif 6, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ בָּאוּ סְתָם וְיֵשׁ לָחוּשׁ שֶׁמָּא בָּאוּ עַל עִסְקֵי נְפָשׁוֹת … אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת … רוֹצִים לָבֹא … אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, שֶׁמָּא יִלְכְּדוּ הָעִיר וּמִשָּׁם תְּהֵא הָאָרֶץ נוֹחָה לִכָּבֵשׁ לִפְנֵיהֶם » → and even if they came without stated purpose and there is reason to fear that they came for matters of lives … desecrate Shabbos for them … wish to come … desecrate Shabbos for them, lest they capture the city and from there the land become easy to conquer before them
- **séif 7, EN** (traduite de l'hébreu (the FR rendering of the chazaka was inverted at the time; the FR has since been corrected to match)) — « שֶׁאָנוּ דָרִין בֵּין הָאֻמּוֹת שֶׁשּׁוֹלְלִין וְהוֹרְגִין … וְלָבֹז … וַחֲזָקָה שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מַעֲמִיד עַצְמוֹ עַל מָמוֹנוֹ, וְיֵשׁ לָחוּשׁ שֶׁמָּא יַעֲמוֹד אֶחָד נֶגְדָּם וְיֵהָרֵג, וַהֲרֵי זֶה עֵסֶק נְפָשׁוֹת » → when we dwell among the nations who plunder and kill … and despoil … and there is a presumption (chazakah) that a person does not hold himself back where his money is concerned, and there is reason to fear lest one stand against them and be killed — and this is a matter of lives
- **séif 7, EN** (portée depuis la page FR (wording « allow » set in the second pass)) — « לִקַּח מָמוֹנוֹ — יַנִּיחַ לָהֶם לִטּוֹל כָּל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ, וְלֹא יְחַלֵּל שַׁבָּת אֲפִלּוּ בְּאִסּוּר דִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, אֶלָּא עַל דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִימָן ש״א » → to take his money — let him allow them to take all that he has, and let him not desecrate Shabbos even by a prohibition of the words of the Sofrim (issur divrei soferim), except in the manner explained in Siman 301
- **séif 7, FR** (traduite de l'hébreu (Sanhedrin 72a, Rashi « ושותק כשרואה שנוטלים ממונו »)) — « וַחֲזָקָה שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מַעֲמִיד עַצְמוֹ עַל מָמוֹנוֹ » → et la ḥazaka est qu'un homme ne reste pas passif quand on lui prend son argent (corrects an existing inverted translation; no new passage)
- **séif 8, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « כְּדֵי לְהַצִּילָם, וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא סָפֵק אִם יַצִּיל … מֵאַחַר שֶׁהוּא חוּץ מִן הַסַּכָּנָה. וְאַף שֶׁרוֹאֶה בְּמִיתַת חֲבֵרוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא סָפֵק וַחֲבֵרוֹ וַדַּאי, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר "וָחַי בָּהֶם", וְלֹא שֶׁיָּבֹא לִידֵי סְפֵק מִיתָה עַל יְדֵי שֶׁיְּקַיֵּם מַה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר "לֹא תַעֲמֹד עַל דַּם רֵעֶךָ" » → in order to save them, and even if it is doubtful whether he will save … since he is outside the danger. And even though he sees his fellow's death, and even though his own case is a doubt while his fellow's is a certainty, nevertheless it is said "va-chai bahem" — and not that he should come to a doubt of death by fulfilling what is said "lo sa'amod al dam re'echa" ("you shall not stand by the blood of your fellow")
- **séif 9, EN** (portée depuis la page FR) — « מִפְּנֵי הַנָּכְרִים לִשְׁבּוֹת בַּמָקוֹם שֶׁהִצִּילוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִימָן ת״ז » → because of the non-Jews, to stay (lishbos) in the place where they rescued, as will be explained in Siman 407

### Siman 330

- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR (sa-fr, déjà complète), relue sur l'hébreu) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵין הַנָּשִׁים הַמִּתְעַסְּקוֹת עִמָּהּ צְרִיכוֹת לְנֵר… אַף עַל פִּי כֵן ; שֶׁאוֹמֶרֶת בְּלִבָּהּ "אִם יֵשׁ דָּבָר…" מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן כְּשֶׁתֵּדַע שֶׁאֵין כַּאן נֵר — יֵשׁ לָחוּשׁ שֶׁתִּסְתַּכֵּן… ; וְאֵין אַחַת מֵאֶלֶף מֵתָה מֵחֲמַת לֵדָה, לְפִיכָךְ ; בּוֹ כְּגוֹן… דֶּרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים… שֶׁהִיא הוֹצָאָה שֶׁלֹּא כְדַרְכָּהּ… ; וְאִם צְרִיכָה לְשֶׁמֶן… כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שכ״ו » → …and even if the women attending to her do not need a lamp, for they could attend to her for all her needs without a lamp. Even so, we kindle on her account, for even though she does not see — her mind is more settled when she knows that there is a lamp here, for she says in her heart, "if there is anything I need, my companions see and do it for me"; whereas when she knows that there is no lamp here — there is reason to fear that she will be endangered by the dread of her heart, for she will fear that perhaps they are not doing properly what she needs. / Nevertheless, since the pains of the yoledes are natural things, and not one in a thousand dies on account of childbirth, the Sages were therefore stringent to vary (shinnui) in whatever can be varied — for example, if they need to bring her a utensil through reshus harabim (the public domain), her companion should bring it to her hanging from her hair, which is carrying out (hotza'ah) not in its usual manner, and involves no Torah prohibition; and likewise in all similar cases. / And if she needs oil — her companion should not smear oil on her own hair and come to her and wring it out; rather, she should bring it in a utensil hanging from her hair, for it is better to carry out in a utensil by means of a variation (shinnui), with no other prohibition, than to add the prohibition of wringing (sechitah), even though wringing hair is forbidden only mi-divrei soferim, as was explained in siman 326:
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu (remplace l'ancienne phrase « it is forbidden for us to deliver non-Jewesses on Shabbos », qui rendait les deux mots changés)) — « כְּגוֹן שֶׁכְּבָר נֶעֱקַר הַוָּלָד לָצֵאת, שֶׁאֵין בְּהוֹצָאָתוֹ מִשּׁוּם עוֹקֵר דָּבָר מִגִּדּוּלוֹ ; וְנוֹתְנִים לָהּ שָׂכָר, שֶׁמֻּתָּר לָהּ לְיַלֵּד נָכְרִיּוֹת בְּחֹל מִשּׁוּם אֵיבָה ; "הַנָּשִׁים שֶׁלָּנוּ… אָסוּר לָנוּ לְיַלֵּד אוֹתָן בְּשַׁבָּת" ; וַאֲפִלּוּ יִשְׁמְעֵאלִית… כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן של״ב… ; אֲבָל הַקָּרָאִי״ם… ; וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּנָכְרִים עוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, אִם אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהִשָּׁמֵט בְּלִי אֵיבָה — מֻתָּר… » → …for example when the child has already been uprooted to come out, so that bringing it out does not involve [the prohibition of] uprooting a thing from where it grows (oker davar mi-gidulo). And even if the Jewess is a recognized midwife and is paid a fee, so that she is permitted to deliver non-Jewesses on a weekday because of eivah (enmity) — nevertheless on Shabbos there is no eivah here, for she can deflect by saying: "our women, who keep Shabbos — it is permitted to deliver them on Shabbos; but yours, who do not keep Shabbos — it is forbidden for us to deliver them on Shabbos". / And even Ishmaelite women, who do not worship idolatry (and it is not forbidden to deliver them on a weekday, as with idolatrous non-Jews, whom [the Sages] forbade because she delivers a son for idolatry — nevertheless on Shabbos) — it is forbidden, because of a melachah done without need, as will be explained in siman 332 (since it is possible to deflect without eivah). / But the Karaites, since they keep Shabbos — it is permitted to deliver them (for a fee, with something that does not involve chillul Shabbos, because of eivah). / And even with idolatrous non-Jews, if it is impossible to deflect without eivah — it is permitted with something that does not involve chillul Shabbos:
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu (« יַזְמִין » rendu « one should prepare », là où l'ancien EN et le FR disent « she »)) — « שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ כֹּחַ לַהֲלוֹךְ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּרְאָה אֶחָד מִכָּל אֵלּוּ — מְחַלְּלִים עָלֶיהָ הַשַּׁבָּת לְכָל צְרָכֶיהָ אֲשֶׁר אֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָן כַּאן בְּלִי עִכּוּב ; מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא דָבָר הַצָּרִיךְ שָׁהוּת וְעִכּוּב ; מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁמַּרְגֶּשֶׁת קְצָת, אֲפִלּוּ בְּסָפֵק ; כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטָרְכוּ לְחַלֵּל שַׁבָּת » → …because she has no strength to walk: as soon as any one of these is seen — we transgress Shabbos for her for all her needs that can be done here without delay. But to call the midwife from place to place, which is a matter requiring time and delay — that is permitted earlier, from when she feels [it] a little, even in doubt. / And it is good to take care that when the woman reaches the 9th month, one should prepare everything from erev Shabbos, so that they will not need to transgress Shabbos:
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu (« חַמִּין » laissé en terme hébreu : le FR dit « eau chaude » puis « le chaud »)) — « מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵבָרֶיהָ מִתְפָּרְקִים וּמְרֻסֶּקֶת הִיא ; לְחִלּוּל, אֶלָּא שֶׁחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ אוֹמְרוֹת שֶׁצְּרִיכָה, אֲפִלּוּ אֵין בָּהֶן חֲכָמָה וְלֹא רוֹפֵא ; שֶׁהַדְּבָרִים שֶׁעוֹשִׂים לַחַיָּה בְּחֹל… יְבִיאוּהָ לִידֵי סַכָּנָה ; לְחִלּוּל ; כִּי הַרְבֵּה יוֹלְדוֹת יֵשׁ שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂין לָהֶן חַמִּין בְּכָל יוֹם. וְכֵן נוֹהֲגִים עַכְשָׁו בִּקְצָת מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁאוֹכְלוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת חַמִּין שֶׁל אֶתְמוֹל » → …because her limbs are dislocated and she is crushed; therefore we transgress Shabbos for her. / Even if she says "I do not need transgression", but her companions say that she does need it — even if there is neither a midwife nor a doctor among them — we do for her all her needs, for the things that are done for a chayah (a woman after childbirth) on a weekday are known to all. / And even if she eats the food of other people — we say that she does not feel [it] at the time, and if she eats cold food or other things that a person in danger does not eat — they will bring her to danger. / But if a midwife or doctor says that she does not need transgression — we listen to them, for there are many women after childbirth for whom chamin (hot things) are not made every day. And so is the practice nowadays in some places, that they eat on Shabbos chamin from the previous day:
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « אֲפִלּוּ חַבְרוֹתֶיהָ אוֹמְרוֹת שֶׁצְּרִיכָה… עַד לָעֶרֶב ; אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן שֶׁחֲכָמָה אוֹ רוֹפֵא… בְּסִמָּן שכ״ח ; וְכָל זֶה כְּשֶׁהִיא אוֹמֶרֶת… מְחַלְּלִין ; אֲבָל אִם חַבְרוֹתֶיהָ אוֹמְרוֹת שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה — אֵין מְחַלְּלִין ; אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן ; שֶׁאָז ; כִּי "לֵב יוֹדֵעַ מָרַת נַפְשׁוֹ", וּכְמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן תרי״ח… ; עָלֶיהָ ; שֶׁכָּל צְרָכָיו נַעֲשִׂים עַל יְדֵי נָכְרִי ; שֶׁאִם הִיא צְרִיכָה לְכָךְ… נִתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן רע״ו » → From the 3rd to the end of the 7th day, if she said "I do not need" — we do not transgress, even if her companions say that she needs, because she is not presumed to be in danger with respect to those things that her companions are accustomed to do for her on a weekday, and it is possible that she can wait until evening. / Unless a midwife or doctor says that she needs, for then we transgress even for other sick people, as was explained in siman 328. / And all this is when she says "I do not need"; but in an unspecified case (stam), when she says nothing and her companions do not know — we transgress. / But if her companions say that she does not need — we do not transgress. / Unless she says "I do need", for then even if a hundred doctors say that she does not need — we transgress, for "the heart knows the bitterness of its soul", as will be explained in siman 618 regarding other sick people. / And from the end of the 7th day onward, even if she said "I do need" — we do not transgress Shabbos for her; rather she is, up to 30 days, like a sick person without danger (choleh she-ein bo sakanah), all of whose needs are done by a non-Jew, except concerning fire (medurah): if she needs it, it is made for her by a Jew, because the cold is a danger for her all 30 days, even in tekufas Tammuz (the summer season). And whether others are permitted to warm themselves by this fire — was explained in siman 276:
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ ג' מֵעֵת לְעֵת עַד סָמוּךְ לָעֶרֶב בְּשַׁבָּת, וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן שִׁבְעָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים » → …even though 3 days me-eis le-eis have not been completed until close to evening on Shabbos. And likewise regarding the seven and the thirty [days]:
- **séif 7, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרֹב הַפְּעָמִים הוּא מֵת קֹדֶם לְאִמּוֹ… כְּמוֹ עַל מִי שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ חֶזְקַת חַיּוּת ; אֲפִלּוּ בְּחֹל ; שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לַוָּלָד לִחְיוֹת, כִּי שֶׁמָּא נִתְעַלְּפָה… וּבְתוֹךְ כָּךְ וַדַּאי מֵת הַוָּלָד » → …And even though most of the time he dies before his mother, nevertheless in pikuach nefesh we do not follow the majority, and sometimes he can live after his mother's death. And even though this fetus never had a presumption of life (chezkas chayus) — we transgress for him out of doubt, as for one who had a presumption of life. / And the reason we do not do so nowadays, even on a weekday, is because we are not expert in [recognizing] the mother's death so quickly that it is still possible for the child to live, for perhaps she has fainted, and if they cut open her belly they will kill her; so they must wait, and in the meantime the child certainly dies:
- **séif 8, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « שֶׁיִּתְקַשֶּׁה הַבָּשָׂר, וְטוֹמְנִין אֶת הַשִּׁלְיָא… הִתִּירוּ חֲכָמִים ; דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁנּוֹלַד ; (כְּגוֹן שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ מִמֶּנָּה בַּעְלָהּ אַחַר שֶׁבָּעַל) ; בֵּין בִּדְבָרִים אֵלּוּ… לְפַקֵּחַ עָלָיו אֶת הַגַּל ; שֶׁאָז בְּוַדַּאי נִגְמַר לְז'… (321 mots, jusqu'à) …כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר) » → …so that the flesh will harden, we bury the afterbirth (shilya) so that the child will be warm, and we cut the umbilical cord (tabbur), for all these things cause him distress if they are not done for him; and since they involve no melachah of the Torah, the Sages permitted them. / …— that is, one born at 9 or at 7 months with certainty (for example, if her husband separated from her after cohabiting)… neither in these things nor even in things whose omission poses a danger to him, such as clearing a heap [of debris] from over him — unless his hair and nails are complete, for then he was certainly completed at 7 [months], only he lingered in coming out of his mother's womb after his completion. And therefore, even if he is certainly an 8-month child… — his law is like that of a certain 7-month child in every respect, and we circumcise him on Shabbos. / But if his hair was deficient and his nails are not complete according to their nature, even if he is [only] a doubtful 8-month child — his law is like that of a certain 8-month child in every respect, even with regard to pikuach nefesh… and he is a nefel (non-viable). / And even handling him (tiltul) is forbidden, just as it is forbidden to handle a stone; and therefore we do not circumcise him on Shabbos. And we do not say "let him be circumcised mimah nafshach (either way)…"; for since it is close to certain that he is an 8-month child, the Sages did not want to permit handling for the sake of his circumcision where it is close to certain that his circumcision is nothing. / And some disagree with this… and likewise we circumcise him on Shabbos mimah nafshach. / But we do not transgress Shabbos for him according to all opinions… / And according to this view, nowadays we circumcise every child, even one born at 8 [months]… and likewise it is permitted to handle him on Shabbos. / (And as for the halachah: in matters mi-divrei soferim, follow the lenient view; therefore one who is lenient regarding handling in accordance with the latter view — has not lost. But regarding circumcision on Shabbos — "shev ve-al ta'aseh adif" (sitting and not acting is preferable), and it is good to be concerned for the words of those who forbid. And even though there is no prohibition in handling him — there is a prohibition in circumcising him on Shabbos without need, just as there is a prohibition in cutting the umbilical cord on Shabbos even according to the latter view, as was explained): [texte intégral dans le &lt;p class="sa-en"&gt; du séif 8]
- **séif 9, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « שֶׁמְּצַעֲרָהּ ; שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהֶחָלָב הַזֶּה הוֹלֵךְ — לְאִבּוּד אֵין בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם מְפָרֵק אֶלָּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, וּמִשּׁוּם צַעֲרָהּ לֹא גָזְרוּ (et « לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל », déjà rendu par « according to all ») » → …because of the pain of the milk that pains her. Likewise she herself may extract by hand the milk that pains her, for since this milk goes to waste, it involves [the prohibition of] mefarek (extracting) only mi-divrei soferim, and because of her pain they did not decree:
- **séif 10, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים? בְּיוֹם הַלֵּדָה, אֲבָל אַחַר כָּךְ אָסוּר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְּבוֹנֶה ; מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ בְּכָךְ גַּם בְּחֹל, וְאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כְּבוֹנֶה בְּשַׁבָּת. אֲבָל תִּינוֹק שֶׁאֵין כּוֹרְכִין אוֹתוֹ בְּחֹל… אָסוּר לְכָרְכוֹ כְּדֵי לְיַשְּׁרָם » → To what does this apply? On the day of the birth; but afterward it is forbidden, because it appears like building (boneh). But to wrap him in his garments in order to straighten his limbs — is always permitted, because that is his usual way even on a weekday, and it does not appear like building on Shabbos. But an infant whom one does not wrap on a weekday, and whose limbs became bent on Shabbos — it is forbidden to wrap him in order to straighten them:
- **séif 11, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, relue sur l'hébreu) — « אַף עַל פִּי שִׁלְּפָעִמים יָקִיא עַל יְדֵי זֶה » → …even though sometimes he may vomit as a result:

### Siman 355

- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁכָּל מְחִצָּה הָעֲשׂוּיָה לְפָחוֹת מֵחָלָל ד' אֵינָהּ מְחִצָּה. וְאֵין חִלּוּק בֵּין אִם יַעֲשֶׂה לָהּ מְחִצָּה זוֹ לְמַטָּה … בְּעִנְיָן זֶה » → for any partition made for a space of less than 4 is not a partition. And there is no difference whether one makes this partition below, attached to it beneath its whole perimeter or attached beneath the perimeter of the hole, or whether one makes it on top of it, above, in this manner
- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אֲבָל אִם עֲשָׂאָהּ לְצֹרֶךְ אַחֵר, אֵין אוֹמְרִים בָּהּ "גּוּד אַחֵת", מִטַּעַם שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר » → But if one made it for another purpose, we do not say gud achis regarding it, for the reason that will be explained.
- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « מִמֶּנָּה … אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַשּׁוֹפָכִין הוֹלְכִים לַחוּץ לְהָלְאָה מִכְּנֶגֶד הַמְּחִצָּה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן לְכָךְ » → to pour out from it through the hole, even though the wastewater goes outward, beyond the line of the partition, since one does not intend this
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁאָנוּ רוֹאִים צְדָדֵי הַדַּף כְּאִלּוּ נִמְשְׁכוּ וְיָרְדוּ לְמַטָּה עַד הַתְּהוֹם, וְנִמְצְאוּ הַמַּיִם שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו מֻקָּפִים מְחִצָּה » → that is, we view the sides of the board as if extended and lowered down to the depths, so that the water beneath it is enclosed by a partition
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹת בִּסְפִינָה מַה שֶּׁיָּכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹת בַּבַּיִת » → because one cannot do on a ship what one can do in a house
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אֶלָּא בְּתוֹכָהּ בִּלְבָד הִיא עֲמֻקָּה עֲשָׂרָה … שֶׁנִּמְצָא כְּשֶׁמְּמַלֵּא דֶּרֶךְ מַעְלָה מֵהַדְּפָנוֹת הוֹלֵךְ הַדְּלִי דֶּרֶךְ מַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה מֵעַל הַמַּיִם » → and only on the inside is it 10 deep. … so that when one draws over the top of the walls the bucket passes above ten above the water
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁלֹּא יָבֹא לְטַלְטֵל מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לְכַרְמְלִית שֶׁלֹּא בְּדֶרֶךְ אֲוִיר מְקוֹם פְּטוּר. וּלְפִיכָךְ … וְכֵן בְּהַחֲזָרָתוֹ) » → so that one does not come to carry from reshus ha-yachid to a karmelis other than through the air of a makom petur. Therefore one need not make a hole in the ziz to draw through it; rather one may draw anywhere on the ship by means of the ziz marker made at one place on the ship. And even those who forbid carrying from reshus ha-yachid to a karmelis even by way of setting down on a makom petur in between, as explained in siman 346 — here, where there is a ziz of any size as a marker, agree that it is permitted, for the reason for the prohibition is only lest one come to carry not by way of a makom petur in between, as explained there, and here one has a marker in the ziz of any size. (And according to those who say it is permitted to carry from reshus ha-yachid to a karmelis by way of setting down on a makom petur, but not through the air of a makom petur, the ziz marker is of no use above ten, just as it is of no use below 10 tefachim. Therefore one must here first set the bucket down on the ziz, which is a makom petur, and afterwards lower it into the water, and likewise when bringing it back)
- **séif 4, EN** (premier passage ; rapport initial tronque a cet endroit, origine (FR ou hebreu) non verifiable ; concorde avec le texte source) — « מִמֶּנָּה … אֲפִלּוּ אִם דָּפְנֵי הַסְּפִינָה אֵינָם גְּבוֹהִים י' טְפָחִים מֵעַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם מִבַּחוּץ … אוֹ זוֹרְקָן … שֶׁשָּׁם הוּא אֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד — כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שמ"ה … וּמִשָּׁם הֵם יוֹרְדִים דֶּרֶךְ דָּפְנוֹתֶיהָ לַיָּם. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין נָחִים כְּלָל עַל הַדֹּפֶן … וּמִכֹּחוֹ הֵם בָּאִים לַיָּם … אֲבָל אִם (אֵינוֹ) רוֹצֶה לִשְׁפּוֹךְ עַל הַדֹּפֶן מַמָּשׁ … עַל דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּמִלּוּי » → from it … even if the ship's walls are not 10 tefachim high above the surface of the water on the outside; … or throws … where it is the air of reshus ha-yachid — as explained in siman 345, that the tops of the partitions of a reshus ha-yachid are reshus ha-yachid — and from there they descend along its walls to the sea. … even though they do not rest on the wall at all but descend immediately to the sea, and that is what one intends — nevertheless, since one does not pour and throw them with one's hands into the sea but onto the wall, and it is by one's force that they come to the sea; and … But if one wishes to pour not onto the wall itself but a little way off, directly into the sea, a ziz marker or a perforated 4-by-4 board is needed here too, and one pours through the hole, in the manner explained for drawing:
- **séif 5, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; libelle « except for » corrige au deuxieme passage) — « בְּלֹא דַף וּבְלֹא זִיז, שֶׁהֲרֵי מֻתָּר לְטַלְטֵל מִכַּרְמְלִית לְכַרְמְלִית. וְלֹא אָמְרוּ שֶׁאֵין כַּרְמְלִית בְּכֵלִים אֶלָּא בִּכְלִי הָעוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים … כַּרְמְלִית הוּא » → without daf and without ziz, since it is permitted to carry from a karmelis to a karmelis. And they did not say that there is no karmelis in vessels except for a vessel standing in reshus ha-rabbim, which is nullified to the reshus ha-rabbim and becomes like it; but when it stands in a karmelis, it is a karmelis:
- **séif 7, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « שֶׁאֵין רֹחַב ד' טְפָחִים לְמַטָּה בְּעוֹד שֶׁלֹּא הִגְבִּיהוּ ג' טְפָחִים מִקַּרְקָעִיתוֹ … אֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ בִּמְחִצּוֹתֶיהָ גֹּבַהּ י' טְפָחִים … כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר … וְכֵן אִם מִלֵּא קָנִים בְּקַרְקָעִיתָן עַד מְקוֹם רָחְבָּן, הֲרֵי הֵן מְחִצּוֹת כְּשֵׁרוֹת (לְמַעְלָה) אִם יֵשׁ בָּהֶן י' מִשָּׁם וּלְמַעְלָה » → such that there is no width of 4 tefachim below until one has risen 3 tefachim from the floor … even if its partitions are 10 tefachim high from the place where they widened to 4 and upward; for since at the narrow place it has no partitions at all — any partition made for a space of less than 4 tefachim is not a partition, as explained above — then from the place where (the partitions) widened to 4 tefachim and upward they are like partitions suspended in the air, which do not have the status of a partition, and we do not say gud achis regarding them unless they were made to permit carrying in water, as will be explained. … Likewise, if one filled their floor with reeds up to the place of their width, they are valid partitions (above) if they have 10 from there upward:
- **séif 8, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « כְּשִׁעוּר כַּרְמְלִית … שֶׁאִם אֵינָן קְשׁוּרוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ … וְאֵין זֶה עֵרוּב רְשֻׁיּוֹת … וְאֵין אוֹמְרִים בְּזֶה "שַׁבָּת כֵּיוָן שֶׁהֻתְּרָה הֻתְּרָה", עַיֵּן סִמָּן (שמ"ט) [שס"ב] » → — the measure of a karmelis … for if they are not tied to each other, they can drift and separate from each other, and a karmelis would interrupt between them, and this is not an eruv reshuyos. … and here we do not say Shabbos, once it was permitted, remains permitted; see siman 362:
- **séif 9, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם פְּטוּר. וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן שֶׁל שְׁנַיִם … וּכְבָר נִתְבָּאֵר שָׁם שֶׁהָעִקָּר כְּהָאוֹסְרִים בְּזֶה » → which is a makom petur. And even if they belong to two persons, it is permitted without an eruv according to those who permit carrying through the air of a makom petur in reshuyos of rabbinic status, as explained in siman 346. And it has already been explained there that the main view is like those who forbid in this case:
- **séif 10, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « עַל דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בִּגְזוּזְטְרָא … לְפִי שֶׁמִּן הַדִּין לֹא הָיָה רָאוּי לְהַתִּיר כְּלָל … אֲבָל מְחִצָּה שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשֵׂית בִּשְׁבִיל כָּךְ, אֵין בָּהּ הֶכֵּר כְּלָל » → in the manner explained for the gezuzṭra, … For by strict law it would not have been fitting to permit at all on account of gud achis, even with a suspended partition below, since regarding partitions suspended on dry land 3 tefachim high off the ground we do not say gud achis, because young goats can pass beneath them, as explained in siman 345 — and here in water, although young goats cannot pass there, fish nevertheless can pass there — and for this reason the gezuzṭra is not reshus ha-yachid unless a window opens onto it. Rather, it is a leniency that the Sages were lenient regarding water to say gud achis, since the passage of the fish is not visible; and they were lenient only when one made the partition for the sake of the water, to permit carrying in it, for one has thereby a marker and a reminder that the water beneath is a karmelis and that carrying is permitted there only by means of a partition. But a partition that was not made for this purpose carries no marker at all:
- **séif 11, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « וַהֲרֵי הֵם כְּחוֹרֵי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד … וְכָל זֶה בְּכַרְמְלִית שֶׁחוּץ לָעִיר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ"ז … כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שי"ב » → and they are like the holes of a reshus ha-yachid, which are judged as reshus ha-yachid; thus one is taking the excrement out from reshus ha-yachid to reshus ha-yachid, and it is by one's force (kocho) that it falls into the water, which is a karmelis, and kocho in a karmelis was not decreed against. The same applies if the outhouse is built so that the excrement falls onto the sides of the wall of the outhouse, which is reshus ha-yachid, and afterwards rolls and falls below into the karmelis. And all this applies in a karmelis outside the city, as will be explained in siman 357. … as explained in siman 312:
- **séif 12, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ"ח … מִטַּעַם שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר, אֲבָל לֹא בְּיַבָּשָׁה … וּלְפִי דִּבְרֵי הַמַּתִּירִים … כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ"ז » → as will be explained in siman 358 … for the reason explained, but not on dry land. … And according to those who permit carrying from reshus ha-yachid to a karmelis through the air of a makom petur, no partition at all is needed, nor a board or reed, for any outhouse that is above ten from the ground of the karmelis. And it has already been explained that the main view is like those who disagree with them. And some say that if there was excrement there from before Shabbos in the karmelis, and one now relieves oneself upon it — it is permitted in any case, even though afterwards it falls off it to the ground of the karmelis, because the excrement from before Shabbos is a makom petur (if it is not 4 by 4) (and it is 3 high, or even if it is not 3 high but is 4 wide, for the public do not step and leap over it but go around it). But according to what was explained in siman 345, that the main view is like those who say there is no makom petur in a karmelis, one should not be lenient in this. And according to what was explained there, that in everyone's view there is no makom petur in reshus ha-yachid, then an outhouse standing between two houses, if they did not make an eruv together — it is forbidden to relieve oneself there according to all views, even if there was excrement there from before Shabbos, unless by means of a board or reed, in which case it is permitted even though it is within the city, because in a courtyard without an eruv they did not decree against one's force (kocho), even though it is within the city, as explained in siman 357:
- **séif 14, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרְחָבָה ד' » → even though it is 4 wide —
- **séif 16, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « אֲפִלּוּ הִיא רְגִילָה לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהּ גַּם בְּחֹל … וּמַה שֶּׁעֲשָׂאוּהָ בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת הוּא מֵאֵיזֶה טַעַם אַחֵר » → even if it is accustomed to use it on weekdays too, since it uses it improperly. Likewise, if they made it for the lower one and not for the upper one — the upper one does not forbid it, unless it made it together with it in partnership. And if they made for both, even if each one was made in partnership (and nevertheless each one uses its own) (and the reasoning of this giluy da'as requires examination), neither forbids the other, and the fact that they made it in partnership is for some other reason:
- **séif 17, EN** (premier passage (rapport initial tronque) ; concorde avec le texte source) — « כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שע"ו, לְפִי שֶׁתַּשְׁמִישׁ שֶׁעַל יְדֵי זְרִיקָה בַּאֲוִיר ד' טְפָחִים בְּרֹחַב אֵינוֹ חָשׁוּב תַּשְׁמִישׁ כְּלָל » → as will be explained in siman 376, because a use made by throwing through an air-space 4 tefachim wide is not considered a use at all:
- **séif 17, EN** (traduite de l hebreu au deuxieme passage (bloquant de l'arbitre), concordante avec la page FR (« il ne peut s'en servir qu'en jetant le seau par un espace de 4 tefahim de large »)) — « הֲרֵי אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהּ אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי זְרִיקַת הַדְּלִי לָאֲוִיר בְּרֹחַב ד' טְפָחִים » → it can use it only by throwing the bucket through the air across a width of 4 tefachim

### Siman 356

- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « לְפִי שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהָאַמָּה אֵינָהּ מִתְעַכֶּבֶת בֶּחָצֵר… וְכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵינָהּ בְּטֵלָה לֶחָצֵר, הֲרֵי הִיא כַּרְמְלִית בֶּחָצֵר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא כַּרְמְלִית קֹדֶם כְּנִיסָתָהּ לֶחָצֵר וְאַחַר יְצִיאָתָהּ מִמֶּנָּה. וְלָכֵן צָרִיךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ מְחִצּוֹת הַמַּפְסִיקוֹת בֵּין הַמַּיִם שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר לְהַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּחוּץ. » → For since the channel does not remain in the courtyard but passes through it from one side to the other, it is not nullified with respect to the courtyard and constitutes a domain of its own, since it is ten deep and four wide, which is the measure that separates one domain from another. And since it is not nullified with respect to the courtyard, it is a &lt;em&gt;karmelis&lt;/em&gt; in the courtyard, just as it is a &lt;em&gt;karmelis&lt;/em&gt; before it enters the courtyard and after it leaves it. Therefore one must make for it partitions that separate between the water in the courtyard and the water outside.
- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR (remplace l'ancienne phrase « And the Alter Rebbe explains: the Sages were lenient… », dont la fin est conservee)) — « וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי כֵן הֵם מְחֻבָּרִים תַּחַת הַמְּחִצָּה אוֹ עַל גַּבָּהּ… כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ״ה. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם כֹּתֶל הֶחָצֵר שֶׁעוֹבֵר שָׁם עֲלֵיהֶם אֵינוֹ מוֹעִיל כְּלוּם אַף אִם הָיָה מְשֻׁקָּע בַּמַּיִם טֶפַח, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֵקֵלּוּ… כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר שָׁם: » → And although they are nevertheless connected beneath the partition or above it, this does not matter, for the Sages were lenient with water, that a suspended partition is effective for it by virtue of &lt;em&gt;gud achis&lt;/em&gt;, as explained in siman 355. Nevertheless, the wall of the courtyard that passes over them there is of no use at all, even if it were submerged a &lt;em&gt;tefach&lt;/em&gt; in the water, because the Sages were lenient with water only with a partition made &lt;em&gt;for the sake of the water&lt;/em&gt; in order to permit carrying within it, as explained there:
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן פְּרוּצוֹת זוֹ לְזוֹ בְּמִלּוּאָן וְאֵין בֵּינֵיהֶן מְחִצָּה » → even though they are open to each other along their full width and there is no partition between them
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם אֵינָהּ עֲמֻקָּה עֲשָׂרָה בִּשְׂפָתָהּ אֶלָּא בְּשִׁפּוּעַ… וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ לָהּ ד' מְחִצּוֹת מִצַּד הָאַמָּה מִכַּאן וְד' מְחִצּוֹת מִצַּד הָאַמָּה מִכַּאן: » → And even if it is not ten deep at its edge but along a slope, if the slope gathers a depth of ten within 4 &lt;em&gt;amos&lt;/em&gt; — it is treated as a partition; but if it gathers so only over more than 4 &lt;em&gt;amos&lt;/em&gt;, it is not treated as a partition, as explained in siman 345. And when it is treated as a partition for the courtyard, it is permitted to carry in it even if the wall of the courtyard was breached along the width of the channel at its entrance and at its exit, and this breach is more than 10 &lt;em&gt;amos&lt;/em&gt; — which would forbid the courtyard, were it not for the excavation of the channel within it, which is treated as a partition for it: it thus has 4 partitions on one side of the channel and 4 partitions on the other:
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וְהִנִּיחַ מָקוֹם פָּנוּי בְּאֶמְצָעִית הַמְּחִצָּה שֶׁל כְּנִיסָה וִיצִיאָה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים וְיוֹצְאִים דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם » → and left an empty space in the middle of the entrance-and-exit partition so that the waters may enter and exit through there
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁפִּרְצָה זוֹ אֵינָהּ אוֹסֶרֶת הֶחָצֵר מִלְּטַלְטֵל בָּהּ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא לָהּ כְּפֶתַח, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שס״ב » → for such a breach does not forbid carrying in the courtyard, since it is for it like an entrance, as will be explained in siman 362
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָמְקוֹ עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחְבּוֹ ד', אֵינוֹ חוֹלֵק רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמוֹ… וְאֵינוֹ נֶאֱסָר מֵחֲמַת שֶׁפָּרוּץ לַיָּם שֶׁהוּא כַּרְמְלִית. » → And although it is ten deep and four wide, it does not constitute a domain of its own, for since it enters the courtyard and does not exit from it, it is like a pit in the courtyard: the partitions of the courtyard permit it, and it is not forbidden on account of being open to the sea, which is a &lt;em&gt;karmelis&lt;/em&gt;.
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהֲרֵי שָׁם נִפְרְצָה הֶחָצֵר בְּמִלּוּאָהּ אוֹ יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר. וְצָרִיךְ לְהַזְהִיר לָרַבִּים… כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שס״ג. (avec, en EN seul, « כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין מְחִצָּה עַל גַּבָּיו בֵּינוֹ לְבֵין הַמַּיִם שֶׁבַּחוּץ ») » → since there is no partition above it between it and the waters outside, for there the courtyard was breached its full width or more than ten. And one must warn the public, who stumble in this in places where a river runs near courtyards.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;But within the courtyard itself it is permitted to carry if the tongue is ten deep within 4 &lt;em&gt;amos&lt;/em&gt;, which is considered a partition. This is when it is far from the seashore, or its banks are very high, such that there is no concern that the sea may deposit a sandbank, its depth be reduced below ten, and people would carry in the courtyard as at first, as will be explained in siman 363.
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַגִּדּוּדִים הַמְּכֻסִּים בַּמַּיִם חֲשׁוּבִים מְחִצָּה לְגַבֵּי הֶחָצֵר… וְלָכֵן אָסוּר לְטַלְטֵל שׁוּם דָּבָר מֵחָצֵר לַבַּית אִם אֵין הַלָּשׁוֹן עָמֹק עֲשָׂרָה: » → because the ridges covered by the water are considered a partition with respect to the courtyard but not with respect to the house: in the courtyard they are sometimes discernible and visible when the waters recede, but in the house they are never discernible and visible. Therefore it is forbidden to carry anything from the courtyard to the house if the tongue is not ten deep:

### Siman 357

- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ נִתְמַלְּאָה הַגּוּמָא מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁתִּקְּנוּ לוֹ חֲכָמִים זֵכֶר לְשַׁבָּת שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁכַּח וְיִשְׁפְּכֵם בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הוּא בְּעַצְמוֹ, שׁוּב אֵין לָחוּשׁ אִם הֵם יוֹצְאִים מֵאֲלֵיהֶם לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים (רַק שֶׁיִּזָּהֵר … כְּמוֹ בִּמְגַלְגֵּל חֵפֶץ מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים) » → and even if the pit was filled from before Shabbos: since the Sages instituted for him a reminder of Shabbos, so that he not forget and pour it into the reshus ha-rabim himself, there is no further concern if the water flows out of its own accord into the reshus ha-rabim (provided only that he take care not to pour it directly adjacent to the reshus ha-rabim away from the pit, in such a way that its exit into the reshus ha-rabim is the direct force of his pouring, for that involves a biblical prohibition, as with one who rolls an object from the reshus ha-yachid into the reshus ha-rabim).
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « וּכְשֶׁהֵם יוֹצְאִים הֲרֵי נִתְקַיְּמָה מַחֲשַׁבְתּוֹ, וְיֵשׁ לִגְזוֹר שֶׁמָּא יִשְׁפְּכֵם בְּעַצְמוֹ הַחוּצָה … אֵין לָחוּשׁ לְכָךְ … (וַאֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹפְכָן סָמוּךְ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַמָּשׁ, אִם הוּא בְּעִנְיָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ "פְּסִיק רֵישֵׁהּ" שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מִיָּד מִכֹּחַ שְׁפִיכָתוֹ מַמָּשׁ) » → and when it flows out his intention is fulfilled, so there is ground to decree lest he pour it outside himself. … there is no concern for this, and … (and even if he pours it directly adjacent to the reshus ha-rabim, if it is in a manner that is not a pesik reishei that it will flow into the reshus ha-rabim immediately by the direct force of his pouring).
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « (אוֹ בֶּחָצֵר סָמוּךְ מַמָּשׁ לִיצִיאָתָן לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, בְּמִתְכַּוֵּן כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים) … וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא בִּשְׁעַת הַגֶּשֶׁם, יֹאמְרוּ מֵי גְשָׁמִים הֵם שֶׁמִּתְמַצִּים וְהוֹלְכִים מִן הַגְּשָׁמִים שֶׁעָבְרוּ » → (or into the courtyard right next to its exit into the reshus ha-rabim, intending that it flow out into the reshus ha-rabim) … And even when it is not raining, they will say it is rainwater that keeps draining from the rains that have passed.
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַסָּאתַיִם שֶׁאָדָם רָגִיל לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּכָל יוֹם אֵין לִגְזוֹר בָּהֶם בְּחָצֵר זוֹ שֶׁהֲרֵי רְאוּיִים לִבָּלַע בָּהּ, וְאִם כֵּן כְּשֶׁשּׁוֹפְכָן אֵינוֹ שׁוֹפְכָן כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לַחוּץ. וְאַף אִם אֵרַע שֶׁיּוֹצְאִין … וְאֵין לִגְזוֹר בָּהּ שֶׁמָּא יִשְׁפְּכֵם הוּא עַצְמוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. לְפִיכָךְ … (וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם צָרִיךְ לִזָּהֵר שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁפּוֹךְ סָמוּךְ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַמָּשׁ כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר) » → because the two se'ah that a person ordinarily uses each day give no ground for a decree in such a courtyard, since they can be absorbed in it; and so when he pours them, he does not pour them in order that they flow outside. And even if it happens that they flow out, that is not his intention, his intention is not fulfilled by this outflow, and there is no ground to decree in it lest he himself pour them into the reshus ha-rabim. Therefore, even if he wishes to use more than two se'ah in it — they did not decree, since it is an uncommon occurrence. And likewise even if it is full of water from before Shabbos, which is also an uncommon occurrence, they did not wish to decree on that account alone, since for the two se'ah that are ordinarily absorbed in it there is no ground to decree (but nevertheless he must take care not to pour directly adjacent to the reshus ha-rabim, as explained).
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu) — « וְאֵין בְּכָל אַחַת מֵהֶן לְבַדָּהּ ד' אַמּוֹת … שֶׁתַּשְׁמִישָׁן נוֹחַ מִזּוֹ לְזוֹ וַהֲרֵי הֵן כִּדְיוּטָא אַחַת » → neither of which alone contains 4 amos … since their use is convenient from one to the other, and they are like a single landing.
- **séif 7, EN** (traduite de l hebreu (la page FR a servi de guide, mais sa glose « seuil de 600 000 traversant requis » n'a pas été portée : l'hébreu dit « כשעור בקיעת הרבים ») ; la phrase anglaise existante sur les mers et fleuves a été réécrite pour suivre la structure de la source) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמִּתְכַּוֵּן שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לַחוּץ, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׁוֹפְכָן בְּיָדָיו לְכַרְמְלִית … כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ״ה. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים … וְלֹא אָמְרוּ שֶׁכֹּחוֹ בְּכַרְמְלִית לֹא גָזְרוּ אֶלָּא בְּיַמִּים וּנְהָרוֹת … אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ כַּרְמְלִית שֶׁבַּיַּבָּשָׁה אֶלָּא שֶׁהִיא חוּץ לָעִיר, שֶׁגַּם כֵּן אֵינָהּ מִתְחַלֶּפֶת … כְּשִׁעוּר בְּקִיעַת הָרַבִּים בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים גְּמוּרָה … וַאֲפִלּוּ בִּזְמַן הַזֶּה … שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ ס' רִבּוֹא. וּמַה שֶּׁהִתִּירוּ בִּגְזוּזְטְרָא … מִשּׁוּם לִכְלוּךְ חֲצֵרוֹ. וְכֵן עִקָּר. וּלְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל בְּחָצֵר שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְעֹרֶבֶת לֹא גָזְרוּ עַל כֹּחוֹ, וּמֻתָּר לִשְׁפּוֹךְ … כֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ מְחִצּוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁמְּחֻסֶּרֶת עֵרוּב » → even though he intends the water to flow outside: since he does not pour it with his hands into the karmelis or directly adjacent to the karmelis, but it reaches the karmelis by his force (kocho), and regarding his force in a karmelis they did not decree, as explained in siman 355. And some hold that there is no distinction here between reshus ha-rabim and karmelis, and that the rule that they did not decree regarding his force in a karmelis was said only of seas and rivers, which are not confused with a reshus ha-rabim, or even of a karmelis on dry land that is outside the city, which likewise is not confused with a full reshus ha-rabim, since the multitude is not usually found outside the city as much as the measure of the multitude's traffic in a full reshus ha-rabim; but a karmelis within the city can be confused with a full reshus ha-rabim. And even nowadays, when some say that we have no full reshus ha-rabim because six hundred thousand do not pass through it, it is nevertheless confused [with one] in a large city that has six hundred thousand. And what they permitted with a balcony (gezuztra) over the water — to pour through the hole, even though the waste water goes by his force beyond the line of the balcony's partition, as explained in siman 355 — is because he does not intend it, as explained there. But here, when he pours into a courtyard that does not have 4 amos in summer, he intends the water to flow outside because of the dirtiness of his courtyard. And so is the main ruling. And according to all opinions, in a courtyard without an eruv they did not decree regarding his force, and it is permitted to pour into this courtyard intending the water to flow from it into that courtyard, even though they did not make an eruv together. And a walled city in which no eruv was made is like a courtyard without an eruv, since it has partitions and lacks only an eruv.
- **séif 8, EN** (portee depuis la page FR, controlee sur l'hebreu ; « [tefachim] » ajouté entre crochets pour « י׳ ») — « וּמוֹשֵׁךְ וְהוֹלֵךְ בְּמֶשֶׁךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הַרְבֵּה … עַל פִּי הַבִּיב שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר … אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַמַּיִם יוֹצְאִים מִיָּד מִיָּדוֹ … כְּמוֹ בֶּחָצֵר. … וְכָל זֶה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. אֲבָל לְכַרְמְלִית — אֲפִלּוּ בְּסִלּוֹן שֶׁל עֵץ מֻתָּר לְפִי סְבָרָא הָרִאשׁוֹנָה … כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שמ״ו » → and which extends a long way along the reshus ha-rabim … at the mouth of the biv that is in the courtyard … even though the water flows immediately from his hand into the reshus ha-rabim beyond the 4 covered amos: because these 4 by 4 of the biv covered in the reshus ha-rabim, which are like cavities of reshus ha-yachid, have the status of a courtyard of 4 by 4, into which it is permitted to pour even in summer, since in 4 by 4 of a biv two se'ah can likewise be absorbed, as in the courtyard. … And all this concerns the reshus ha-rabim. But for a karmelis — even with a wooden silon it is permitted according to the first opinion. And although this is not the main view, one may nevertheless rely on their words to be lenient with a gutter that pours above ten [tefachim] into a karmelis: it is permitted to pour into it even intending that the water descend into the karmelis, since it passes through the airspace of a makom petur, for some permit this in a karmelis, as explained in siman 346.

### Siman 358

- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ כֵּלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בֶּחָצֵר וְהִכְנִיסָם לַבַּיִת אוֹ לְתוֹכוֹ — אָסוּר לְטַלְטְלָם מִזֶּה לָזֶה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יַנִּיחֵם בֶּחָצֵר בֵּינְתַיִם » → (and even utensils that rested in the courtyard and were brought into the house or into it — it is forbidden to carry them from one to the other unless he sets them down in the courtyard in between)
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וַאֲפִלּוּ הֶחָצֵר הוּא שֶׁל אָדָם אֶחָד, וְהַקַּרְפֵּף וְכַיּוֹצֵא בוֹ מִמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁלֹּא הֻקְּפוּ לְדִירָה הוּא שֶׁל אַחֵר, וְלֹא עֵרְבוּ יַחַד — מֻתָּר לְטַלְטֵל מִזֶּה לְזֶה כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שע״ב » → And even if the courtyard belongs to one person, and the karpef or a similar place that was not enclosed for dwelling belongs to another, and they did not make an eruv together — one may carry from one to the other, as will be explained in siman 372.
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « עַל דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שמ״ה ... וּמֻתָּר לְטַלְטֵל מֵהַגַּג לְקַרְפֵּף וְכַיּוֹצֵא בוֹ, כְּמוֹ מֵהֶחָצֵר וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהּ לְקַרְפֵּף » → in the manner explained in siman 345 ... And one may carry from the roof to a karpef and the like, just as from the courtyard and the like to a karpef:
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « בַּיּוֹם וּבַלַּיְלָה ... (אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה הַפֶּתַח פָּתוּחַ מִכְּבָר) וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִקִּיף עוֹד ג' מְחִצּוֹת סָמוּךְ לְכֹתֶל הַבַּיִת שֶׁבּוֹ הַפֶּתַח ... שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהוּא פָּתוּחַ לְבֵית דִּירָה, חָשׁוּב כְּבֵית דִּירָה כְּמוֹ חֲצַר הַבַּיִת. רַק שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁבִּשְׁעַת עֲשִׂיַּת מְחִצּוֹתָיו תְּהֵא עֲשִׂיָּתָן לְדִירָה » → by day and by night ... (or the doorway was already open beforehand) and afterwards enclosed three more partitions adjoining the wall of the house in which the doorway is ... for since it is open to a dwelling-house, it is regarded as a dwelling-house, like the courtyard of a house. Only, it is required that at the time its partitions are made, they be made for dwelling:
- **séif 7, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « לִבְנוֹת בּוֹ וְלִפְתּוֹחַ לוֹ אַחַר כָּךְ, אֵין זֶה מוֹעִיל כְּלוּם. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁכָּל מְחִצּוֹתָיו תְּהֵא עֲשִׂיָּתָן לְדִירָה ... הֲרֵי זֶה גַּם כֵּן מֻקָּף לְדִירָה » → to build in it and to open to it afterwards — this is of no avail at all. Nevertheless, it is not required that all its partitions be made for dwelling; rather, even if he enclosed it with three complete partitions, only that the fourth was breached more than ten amos — for such a breach nullifies the partitions, as will be explained in siman 362 — and before he fenced in the excess beyond ten he made in it a sukkah or a dwelling-house, or a doorway from a dwelling-house — this is mukaf le-dira. And if he built a dwelling next to this breach, and fences in the excess beyond ten, leaving the ten, which are a doorway from this dwelling — this too is mukaf le-dira.
- **séif 8, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « כְּמוֹ בְּפוֹרֵץ בְּבַת אַחַת וְגוֹדֵר אַחַר כָּךְ ... כְּמוֹ הַגּוֹדֵר לְדִירָה » → as with one who breaches at once and fences afterwards ... like one who fences in for dwelling:
- **séif 9, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « בְּגֹבַהּ פָּחוֹת מִי' טְפָחִים ... בְּסִמָּן שס״ב (premiere opinion detaillee) ; וְיֵשׁ חוֹלְקִין עַל זֶה ... אֲבָל בְּצַד הַחוּץ אֵין מוֹעִיל כְּלוּם (deuxieme opinion detaillee) » → at a height of less than ten tefachim, the earth being four tefachim wide or more, over a length of more than ten amos along the length of the wall; and if the wall is not twenty tefachim high, so that there are not ten tefachim in the height of the wall from the height of the earth upward — it is considered as if breached there, in the place of the earth. For there is a foothold from the ground onto the earth, since it is not ten tefachim high, and anything less than ten tefachim is as if breached and completely open; and from the earth there is a foothold onto the wall, which there is not ten high, so it is as if completely breached there. And the earth should lie thus for at least one Shabbos, and before he clears it away from there he should make within it a dwelling-house or open to it a doorway from a dwelling-house; and afterwards, when he clears it away from there and the wall becomes ten high, this is as if he built it anew for dwelling. And although this is a partition that comes into being by itself, through the clearing away of the earth outside it, this does not matter, as will be explained in siman 362. / And others dispute this, saying that his having designated the earth to lie there for one Shabbos is of no use at all to nullify the wall so that it be considered breached, since he intends to clear it away from there later. And the earth can nullify the wall only if he designated it to lie there forever — and if so, with what will he fence it in for dwelling? Therefore earth is of no use unless ... for then it becomes a new partition and nullifies the first partition beside it over a length of more than ten amos, as will be explained. And then it need not be four tefachim wide, nor need it be placed on both sides of the wall, but on its inner side only; on the outer side, however, it is of no use at all.
- **séif 10, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר לְמַעְלָה בָּרְחָבָה שֶׁאֲחוֹרֵי הַבָּתִּים, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהִיא יְתֵרָה מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם כַּרְמְלִית הִיא, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שנ״ט » → as was explained above regarding the rechavah behind the houses, that when it exceeds a bes se'asayim it is a karmelis, and as will be explained in siman 359:
- **séif 12, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « לְפִיכָךְ אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ הָאִילָנוֹת גְּבוֹהִים עֲשָׂרָה וּרְחָבִים ד', שֶׁחוֹלְקִין רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמָן, אֵין מְמַעֲטִין ... אֲבָל דָּבָר אַחֵר שֶׁאֵינוֹ צֹרֶךְ הַגִּנָּה (וְאֵין דַּרְכּוֹ לִהְיוֹת בְּתוֹכָהּ) — מְמַעֶטֶת » → Therefore, even if the trees were ten high and four wide, so that they constitute a reshus of their own, they do not reduce. ... But something else that is not a need of the garden (and is not usually found in it) — does reduce:
- **séif 14, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁתִּהְיֶה כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֵינָהּ, וְיִהְיֶה מֻקָּף לְדִירָה עַל יְדֵי מְחִצָּה זוֹ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁעָשָׂה בּוֹ בֵּית דִּירָה אוֹ פָּתַח בּוֹ פֶּתַח מִבֵּית דִּירָה קֹדֶם שֶׁעָשָׂה מְחִצָּה זוֹ ... וְאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מוֹסִיף עַל עֹבִי הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, וְאֵין זֶה מוֹעִיל אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נִתְמַעֵט אֲוִירוֹ מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר לְקַמָּן » → so that it be as though it were not, and the place be mukaf le-dira by means of this partition, e.g. he had made a dwelling-house in it or opened in it a doorway from a dwelling-house before making this partition ... and it merely adds to the thickness of the first; and this is of no avail unless its open space has thereby been reduced below a bes se'asayim, as will be explained below:
- **séif 16, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « כֵּיוָן שֶׁמְּחִצּוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת מִלְּמַטָּה קַיָּמוֹת, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּגְבִּיהָן ... אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמְּחִצָּה הָעֲשׂוּיָה מֵאֵלֶיהָ הִיא ... וְשָׁם אָנוּ אוֹמְרִים גּוּד אַסֵּק ... וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא עָשָׂה אֶלָּא מְחִצָּה אַחַת בְּיוֹתֵר מֵעֲשָׂרָה » → since the original lower partitions remain, only he raises them ... even though it is a partition that came into being by itself ... and although there we apply gud asseq from its partitions upward above its edge all around, so that there are partitions upon partitions — nevertheless, since he dwells in the air of these upper partitions, which he has now made for dwelling, this is mukaf le-dira, even if he made only one partition of more than ten:
- **séif 17, EN** (portee depuis la page FR (au plus pres de l'hebreu la ou le FR interprete, voir points_a_relire)) — « שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הוּא לְהִסְתּוֹפֵף בְּצֵל הָאִילָנוֹת תָּמִיד ... לְפִי שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לָדוּר בִּזְרָעִים ... כְּאִלּוּ לֹא הֻקַּף כְּלָל לְדִירָה ; puis tout le reste du seif, de « נִזְרַע מִעוּטוֹ » a « לְטַלְטֵל מֵהַזָּרוּעַ לַבַּיִת אוֹ לְהֵפֶךְ » » → for it is usual to shelter in the shade of the trees constantly ... since it is not the way to dwell among crops ... as if it had not been enclosed for dwelling at all. / If a minority of it was sown: if the sown area is only a bes se'asayim — one may carry in all of it; [...] And one should be stringent in accordance with the first view. / But if the sown minority is less than a bes se'asayim — one may carry even from within it to the house and from the house into it. [...] / And if the sown minority is more than a bes se'asayim [...] / And all this is in a karpef larger than a bes se'asayim that was sown. [...] And one should be stringent in all this. / Therefore, one who has a sown garden in his courtyard [...] / And if the sown area is the minority of the courtyard: [...] one may be lenient even to carry from the sown area to the house or vice versa: (texte integral dans le sa-en du seif 17 de niveau-4-daat-harav-en.html ; il remplace « If a minority was sown — this is a complex law; see the full text on Sefaria. »)
- **séif 18, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אֶלָּא, אִם הַזָּרוּעַ הוּא יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם — אָסוּר לְטַלְטֵל מֵחָצֵר לְתוֹכוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בְּתוֹךְ ד' אַמּוֹת. וְאִם הוּא בֵּית סָאתַיִם אוֹ פָּחוֹת — מֻתָּר בְּכֵלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בּוֹ אוֹ בֶּחָצֵר, אֲבָל לֹא בְּכֵלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בַּבַּיִת ... כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר » → only, if the sown area is more than a bes se'asayim — it is forbidden to carry from the courtyard into it, even within four amos. And if it is a bes se'asayim or less — it is permitted with utensils that rested in it or in the courtyard, but not with utensils that rested in the house. ... as was explained:
- **séif 19, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁאֵין לְךָ דִּירָה מְעֻלָּה מִזּוֹ. וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁנִּתְפַּשְּׁטוּ שָׁם הוּא יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם, אֲפִלּוּ הֵם עֲמֻקִּים הַרְבֵּה ... אֲבָל אִם אֵין בְּעָמְקָם עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים ... וְאֵין שְׁאָר הַקַּרְפֵּף נֶאֱסָר מֵחֲמַת שֶׁפָּרוּץ לוֹ בְּמִלּוּאוֹ » → for there is no finer dwelling than this; and even if the place over which it spread is more than a bes se'asayim, even if it is very deep. ... But if its depth is not ten tefachim, it is like mud and puddles (tit ve-rekak), which do not constitute a reshus of their own. And when its depth is ten tefachim, if the ten tefachim are gathered within a span of four amos — the water itself is regarded as a partition between it and the rest of the karpef, as was explained in simanim 345 and 356, and the rest of the karpef is not forbidden on account of being breached to it in its full width:
- **séif 20, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « (שֶׁאָנוּ רוֹאִין אֶת פִּי הַתִּקְרָה כְּאִלּוּ הִיא מְחִצָּה יוֹרֶדֶת וְסוֹתֶמֶת וּמַפְסֶקֶת בֵּין הַב' בֵּית סְאִין לַשְּׁלִישִׁית) ... (שֶׁאֵין אוֹמְרִים בּוֹ "פִּי תִקְרָה יוֹרֵד וְסוֹתֵם" כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן ש[ס]"א, כַּאן הֵקֵלּוּ) » → (for we view the edge of the roofing as though it were a partition descending and sealing, separating the two bes se'in from the third) ... (regarding which we do not say "the edge of the roof descends and seals", as will be explained in siman 361 — here they were lenient)
- **séif 21, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « הַקַּרְפֵּף ... וְהֶחָצֵר מֻתֶּרֶת אִם פִּרְצָה זוֹ אֵינָהּ לָהּ בְּמִלּוּאָהּ ... כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן ש״ס) » → the karpef [is forbidden] ... And the courtyard is permitted if this breach is not in its full width and is not more than ten amos (for instance, where the karpef is narrow here and further on gradually widens; for otherwise its length would exceed twice its width, and one may not carry in it except within four amos even if it was not breached at all). / But if it was not breached in its full width, but wall-stubs (gipufin) remained on either side, the place of the breach does not add to it, since the inner edge of the gipufin descends and seals, and from their inner edge outward it is a reshus of its own — for which reason it is forbidden to carry from the house to the threshold if there is no lintel four wide above it, as was explained in siman 346. / (And even if it was breached in its full width — it is forbidden only if the walls along its length protrude and enter into the space of the courtyard, and are three tefachim away there from the walls along the courtyard's length; for otherwise it is permitted by means of the gipufin of the courtyard, as will be explained in siman 360):
- **séif 22, EN** (traduite de l'hebreu (le FR rend ce dernier membre par « la première cloison ne lui sera désormais d'aucun secours », lecture equivalente en substance)) — « שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית אַחַר פְּתִיחַת הַפֶּתַח, שֶׁעַל יָדֶיהָ בָּטְלָה הַמְּחִצָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה עֲשִׂיָּתָהּ לְדִירָה ... וְתוֹעִיל לוֹ עַכְשָׁו מְחִצָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה » → made after the opening of the doorway, through which the first partition, which had not been made for dwelling, was nullified ... so that the first partition would now be effective for it:

### Siman 360

- **séif 1, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וְאָז מֻתָּר אֲפִלּוּ עָשָׂה כֵּן בְּיִשּׁוּב, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצּוֹת גְּמוּרוֹת » → In that case it is permitted even if he did so in an inhabited area, although he could have made complete partitions.
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לָהֶם לְתַשְׁמִישׁ כְּלֵיהֶם וּבְהֶמְתָּם » → that they do not need for the use of their utensils and their animals
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא פָּרוּץ בְּמִלּוּאוֹ לְמָקוֹם הָאָסוּר, דְּהַיְנוּ לְבֵית סָאתַיִם הַפָּנוּי » → since it is &lt;em&gt;perutza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt; (breached along its full width) to a forbidden place, namely to the empty &lt;em&gt;beis sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt;.
- **séif 2, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר בֵּית סָאתַיִם לָהֶם פָּנוּי, שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לָהֶם אֶלָּא ד' סְאִין … וּלְכָל אֶחָד יֵשׁ לוֹ סָאתַיִם. וְיֵשׁ חוֹלְקִין. וּלְעִנְיַן הֲלָכָה, בְּדִבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים הַלֵּךְ אַחַר הַמֵּקֵל » → even though an empty &lt;em&gt;beis sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt; remains to them, since they need only four &lt;em&gt;se'in&lt;/em&gt; … and each one has two &lt;em&gt;se'in&lt;/em&gt;. And there are those who disagree. And as for &lt;em&gt;halacha&lt;/em&gt;, in rabbinic matters one follows the lenient.
- **séif 3, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן צְרִיכִים לְיוֹתֵר » → even though they need more.
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וְהוֹאִיל וְהֻתְּרָה — הֻתְּרָה » → and since it was permitted, it remains permitted (&lt;em&gt;hoil ve-hutra hutra&lt;/em&gt;).
- **séif 4, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהַשַּׁבָּת גּוֹרֶמֶת » → for it is Shabbos that determines.
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « וְהַחִיצוֹנִים אֵינָם פְּרוּצִים בְּמִלּוּאָם לָאֶמְצָעִי אֶלָּא יֵשׁ לָהֶם גִּפּוּפִים עוֹדְפִים עָלָיו מִכַּאן וּמִכַּאן » → while the outer ones are not &lt;em&gt;perutzos bimlu'an&lt;/em&gt; to the middle one but have projections (&lt;em&gt;gipufim&lt;/em&gt;) extending beyond it on either side
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR (sauf « corner nook » pour קרן זויות, traduit de l'hebreu : la page FR dit « cours communes »)) — « מוֹשְׁלִים בָּאֶמְצָעִי, דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁאָנוּ רוֹאִים כְּאִלּוּ הֵם גַּם כֵּן דָּרִים בְּתוֹכוֹ, וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּיַחַד, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין מְחִצָּה לַחֲצוֹץ בֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָם, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּפְרַץ לָהֶם בְּמִלּוּאוֹ, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּקֶרֶן זָוִיּוֹת לָהֶם, שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹלֵק רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמוֹ » → the residents of the outer ones prevail over the middle one, that is, we regard them as also dwelling in it, and so there are three together, since there is no partition to separate between it and them, as it is breached to them along its full width; it is like a corner nook (&lt;em&gt;keren zaviyos&lt;/em&gt;) for them, which does not constitute a domain of its own. (remplace l'ancienne condensation « are considered as also dwelling in the middle »)
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « כֵּיוָן שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ בּוֹ שַׁיָּרָא. וְאַף בַּחִיצוֹנִים מֻתָּרִים לְטַלְטֵל, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם נֶחְשָׁבִים כְּחָצֵר לְהָאֶמְצָעִי, שֶׁהוּא בֵּית דִּירָה לָהֶם, שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ בּוֹ שַׁיָּרָא » → since they have become a &lt;em&gt;shayara&lt;/em&gt; in it. And they may carry even in the outer ones, because these are considered as a courtyard to the middle one, which is a dwelling for them, since they have become a &lt;em&gt;shayara&lt;/em&gt; in it.
- **séif 5, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהֲרֵי הֵם אֵינָם רְשׁוּת אַחַת זֶה עִם זֶה, מִפְּנֵי גִּפּוּפֵיהֶם הַמַּפְסִיקִין בֵּינֵיהֶם. וְאִם הֶקֵּף אֶחָד מֵהֶם יוֹתֵר מִסָּאתַיִם, גַּם הָאֶמְצָעִי אָסוּר, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא פָּרוּץ בְּמִלּוּאוֹ לְמָקוֹם הָאָסוּר. אֲבָל הַחִיצוֹן הַשֵּׁנִי, שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא סָאתַיִם, מֻתָּר » → since they are not one domain with each other, because of their projections that separate between them. And if the enclosure of one of them is more than &lt;em&gt;sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt;, the middle one is also forbidden, since it is &lt;em&gt;perutza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt; to a forbidden place. But the second outer one, which contains only &lt;em&gt;sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt;, is permitted.
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ הַחִיצוֹנִים פְּרוּצִים בְּמִלּוּאָם לָאֶמְצָעִי, וְהָאֶמְצָעִי יֵשׁ לוֹ גִּפּוּפִים עוֹדְפִים עֲלֵיהֶם מִכַּאן וּמִכַּאן » → so that the outer ones are &lt;em&gt;perutzos bimlu'an&lt;/em&gt; to the middle one, and the middle one has projections (&lt;em&gt;gipufim&lt;/em&gt;) extending beyond them on either side
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהֲרֵי דִּיּוּרֵי הַחִיצוֹנִים אֵינָם בָּאֶמְצָעִי … וְאִם אֶחָד מֵהַחִיצוֹנִים יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם — הוּא לְבַדּוֹ אָסוּר, וְהָאֶמְצָעִי אֵינוֹ נֶאֱסָר, כֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ גִּפּוּפִין הַמַּפְסִיקִין, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר הַחִיצוֹן הַשֵּׁנִי » → since the residents of the outer ones are not in the middle one, as it is not breached along its full width; rather, the middle one is in each of them, since they are breached to it along their full width, and so there are in each of them only two — he and the middle one — for the outer ones do not join with each other because of the projections of the middle one that separate between them. And if one of the outer ones is more than &lt;em&gt;beis sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt; — it alone is forbidden, and the middle one does not become forbidden, since it has separating projections, and needless to say the second outer one.
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁהִיא פִּרְצָה אוֹסֶרֶת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּתְבָּאֵר בְּסִמָּן שס״ב. אֲבָל אִם הָאֶמְצָעִי יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם — גַּם הַחִיצוֹנִים אֲסוּרִים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהֵם פְּרוּצִים לוֹ בְּמִלּוּאָם » → which is a forbidding breach, as will be explained in siman 362. But if the middle one is more than &lt;em&gt;beis sa'asayim&lt;/em&gt; — the outer ones are also forbidden, since they are breached to it along their full width.
- **séif 6, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « אוֹ שְׁנַיִם בְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהַחִיצוֹנִים וְיָחִיד בָּאֶמְצָעִי … שֶׁאָנוּ רוֹאִים כְּאִלּוּ דִּיּוּרֵי הָאֶמְצָעִי הֵם בְּכָל אֶחָד מֵהֶם … לְפִי סְבָרָא הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁנִּתְבָּאֵר » → or two in each of the outer ones and one in the middle … for we regard the residents of the middle one as being in each of them, and so they are three together in each of them. And in the middle one too all their needs are permitted, because it is considered as a courtyard to the outer ones, according to the first opinion explained.
- **séif 7, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « שֶׁאִם לֹא כֵן אֵין זוֹ פִּרְצָה בְּמִלּוּאָהּ … אֲזַי נִכָּר וְנִרְאֶה שֶׁהַגִּפּוּפִים הַלָּלוּ אֵינָם מִכֹּתֶל רָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּצָר (passage de 171 mots) » → for otherwise it is not a &lt;em&gt;pirtza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt;; for a breach is called &lt;em&gt;pirtza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt; only if the entire width-wall of a &lt;em&gt;karpef&lt;/em&gt;, courtyard or house is breached. But if the width-wall was longer than the hollow of the width and protruded a &lt;em&gt;tefach&lt;/em&gt; on this side and a &lt;em&gt;tefach&lt;/em&gt; on that side, and it was breached to the measure of the hollow, and there remained of it the strip (&lt;em&gt;pas&lt;/em&gt;) of a &lt;em&gt;tefach&lt;/em&gt; on this side and the strip of a &lt;em&gt;tefach&lt;/em&gt; on that side — even though the strips do not protrude inward into the hollow between the long walls but outward — this is not a &lt;em&gt;pirtza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt;, since small projections (&lt;em&gt;gipufim&lt;/em&gt;) remained of the breached wall. And even though these projections are not visible to one standing inside the &lt;em&gt;karpef&lt;/em&gt;, courtyard or house that is breached along its full width, since they are visible to one standing outside — they are judged as strips (&lt;em&gt;pasin&lt;/em&gt;) for the breach, just as a &lt;em&gt;lechi&lt;/em&gt; visible from outside and not from inside is judged as a &lt;em&gt;lechi&lt;/em&gt; for a &lt;em&gt;mavoi&lt;/em&gt;, as will be explained in siman 363. And so here too, to one standing outside the short &lt;em&gt;karpef&lt;/em&gt;, that is, inside the wide &lt;em&gt;karpef&lt;/em&gt;, these wide projections extending on either side of the breach appear as if they were remnants and projections of the width-wall of the short one, and it was breached only in its middle. This is not so when the long walls of the short one protrude and enter into the hollow of the wide one: then it is evident and visible that these projections are not of the width-wall of the short one.
- **séif 7, EN** (portee depuis la page FR) — « הַבּוֹלְטִים שָׁמָּה הֵם … בְּרֹחַב … כְּגוֹן שֶׁגִּפּוּפִים אֵלּוּ יֵשׁ בְּאֹרֶךְ כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם ג' טְפָחִים … וְאֵין כַּאן פִּרְצָה בְּמִלּוּאָהּ » → that protrude there … in width … for example when each of these projections is 3 &lt;em&gt;tefachim&lt;/em&gt; long. But if they are not 3 &lt;em&gt;tefachim&lt;/em&gt; away from them, they are as if joined (&lt;em&gt;lavud&lt;/em&gt;) and attached there alongside the long walls of the wide one, and the air between them is as if closed. It follows that the short &lt;em&gt;karpef&lt;/em&gt; has projections there, namely from the ends of its long walls to the long walls of the wide one, which are less than 3 &lt;em&gt;tefachim&lt;/em&gt; on this side and less than 3 &lt;em&gt;tefachim&lt;/em&gt; on that side, and there is no &lt;em&gt;pirtza bimlu'oh&lt;/em&gt; here.
- **séif 8, EN** (portee depuis la page FR (« amos » vient de la page FR ; l'hebreu dit seulement « מעשר »)) — « כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינָם פְּרוּצִים זֶה לְזֶה בְּמִלּוּאָם, וְאֵין בְּפִרְצָה יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר » → since they are not &lt;em&gt;perutzos bimlu'an&lt;/em&gt; to one another, and the breach is not more than ten &lt;em&gt;amos&lt;/em&gt;.


## 4. Remarques restantes des arbitres (non bloquantes)


### Siman 1

- La תמצית HE du séif 7 omet toujours la dernière proposition du séif. L'agent a complété la תמצית des séifs 8 et 10 pour la mettre en harmonie avec le FR et l'EN, mais pas celle du 7 : le traitement est inégal d'un bloc à l'autre. C'est un résumé déclaré, qui ne contredit rien, donc ce n'est pas bloquant. L'agent l'a transmis au Rav.
- Séif 18, FR et EN : le « וגו' » de la source est remplacé, sans marque, par la suite du verset (« devant l'Éternel » / « before the Lord »). C'est bien le texte de Lévitique 1:11, et la suite du séif cite « צָפוֹנָה לִפְנֵי ה' ». Pour une traduction strictement littérale, il faudrait « …au nord, etc. ». Ce défaut existait avant les finitions et il est mineur.
- Séif 1, FR et EN : la formule finale « בּוֹרְאוֹ יִתְבָּרֵךְ וְיִתְעַלֶּה » est rendue sans « béni et exalté ». C'est une omission de pure forme, qui existait avant les finitions et ne change pas le sens.
- Point pour le Rav, hors périmètre, à lui transmettre avec ceux du rapport : la תמצית HE du séif 8 garde entre guillemets « ״התורה והתפילה צריך להיות בשמחה — עבדו את ה׳ בשמחה״ ». Ce n'est pas un verbatim : le tiret remplace « כמו שכתוב ». Ce texte n'a pas été touché par les finitions.

### Siman 3

- Séif 14, FR/EN/HE : la traduction omet la troisième proposition, « ואפילו מי שעושה כל מעשיו בימין אלא שכותב בשמאל — לא יקנח בשמאל שכותב בה, מפני שבה קושר תפילין על הימין, כמו שיתבאר בסימן כז סעיף ט ». Seul le chidouch l'évoque (« il déploie… celui qui écrit de la gauche »), sans en donner la règle. Condensation antérieure, laissée par les finitions.
- Séif 6 et séif 24, trois langues : propositions condensées dans le chidouch au lieu d'être traduites. Au 6, la raison « דמתוך שהוא דוחק את עצמו לגדולים הוא בא לידי קטנים… הנתזים למרחוק » devient « la nécessité matérielle pouvant amener un autre besoin » ; « la mesure nécessaire » est un ajout. Au 24, la seconde phrase (« ואם נצרך לקטנים… ועל לא יהיה בך עקר ») n'est rapportée que par le chidouch. Rien d'inversé, mais la traduction est incomplète.
- Séif 18, trois langues : la traduction s'arrête après « sauf qu'il n'a pas à s'éloigner ». Les cas « אם אין שם אדם… אפילו ברשות הרבים / אפילו נכרי אסור / להשתין מותר אפילו ביום בפני רבים » ne sont décrits que dans le chidouch. La condensation n'est pas déclarée comme celles des séifs 20 à 23.
- Séif 16, trois langues : deux portées ambiguës. « herbes sèches tranchantes / sharp dry grasses / בעשבים יבשים חדים » peut se lire comme une restriction à certaines herbes, alors que la source dit des herbes sèches, « שהם חדים ». De même, « une matière… qui n'est pas nocive / which is not harmful » fait de « ואינו מזיק » une condition restrictive, quand la source dit que ce n'est pas nocif. Antérieur.
- Séif 12, HE, première clause : « ואף ששומע קול עיטושו » omet le sujet « חברו », ce qui rend ambigu qui entend. La seconde clause l'a bien.
- Séif 25, FR/EN : « la moindre impureté / the slightest impurity » rend mal « צואה » ; « impureté » évoque la tum'ah. Le renvoi « כמו שיתבאר בסימן עו סעיף ו, עיין שם » devient une parenthèse interprétative, « (lien avec le siman 76, sur la נקיות requise pour la tefila et la qedoucha) », sans le séif 6.
- Séif 4, trois langues : la répétition « וְיַעֲמֹד וְיֵשֵׁב, וְיַעֲמֹד וְיֵשֵׁב » n'est rendue qu'une fois. Détail mineur.

### Siman 8

- Paragraphe HE (séifs 13, 19, 20, 27) : quatre leçons du paragraphe hébreu viennent maintenant de l'édition Kehot non vocalisée, alors que le .sa-he imprimé juste au-dessus (Kehot vocalisée, celle de la porte) porte une autre leçon. Le paragraphe s'écarte donc de son propre texte source. Le sens n'est pas touché. Le rapport parle de « mot faux » (19) et de « comme dans la source » (20) : c'est inexact pour l'édition de la page. Il faut revenir aux leçons du .sa-he.
- Séif 6 FR, défaut antérieur : « בשעת לבישה » est rendu par « au moment du vêtement ». C'est le même défaut que la finition a corrigé au séif 21 (« besoin du vêtement » → « besoin du port »). Mettre « au moment où on le revêt » ou « au moment du port (לבישה) ». L'EN dit bien « at the time of wearing ».
- Séif 2, seif-preview, défaut antérieur, dans les trois langues : il reste un guillemet ASCII collé à un mot hébreu, et ce guillemet est refermé par un geresh, ce qui donne « בְּ'פַתָּח׳ ». Le .sa-he porte « בְּ'פַתָּח' », guillemets simples de citation. Il faut uniformiser, par exemple « בְּפַתָּח » sans guillemets ou une paire cohérente.
- Séif 26 FR, défaut antérieur mineur : dans « מַה שֶּׁדַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁיַּחֲזֹר וְיִלְבָּשֶׁנּוּ », le « שיחזור » (de nouveau) n'est pas rendu. Le FR dit « qu'il ait eu l'intention de le revêtir », l'EN dit bien « his intention to put it on again ».

### Siman 11
